====================================================================================================
QUESTION: What is paid if the insured dies by suicide within 2 years of the issue date?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: Only the premiums paid are returned, without interest.
EVIDENCE: 1.3 EXCLUSION - SUICIDE: If death occurs by suicide within 2 years of the issue date, we will pay only the premiums paid, without interest.
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What happens to the payout in a life insurance policy if the insured dies by suicide within two years of the issue date?
reasoning: This query is asking about a specific clause in a life insurance policy: whether a death by suicide within two years of the policy's issue date results in a payout. This is a well-known provision in life insurance, often referred to as the "suicide clause" or "suicide exclusion." The answer depends on the policy terms, and the key fact to determine is whether the insurer pays out in such a scenario. The query is focused on a single, specific policy condition, so it requires only one lookup: the standard rule regarding suicide within the first two years of a policy.
